# Card review sheet

Generated 2026-11-14 by `npm run cards:review`. Do not edit by hand — edit the
source modules under `src/content` and regenerate. Each row is one card;
the Review column is intentionally blank so you can mark it up.

| ☐ | Leech Check | `dungeon.brewery-quality-control` | group | The drawer checks everyone for leeches. Anyone with one takes a sip to get it off. |  |
| ☐ | Frog Jump | `dungeon.brewery-name-a-beer` | group | Glasses up. The drawer croaks three times; the last glass back on the table drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | The Fireplace | `dungeon.the-compressor` | group | A warm fire in a cold tower. Everyone huddles in and sips. |  |
| ☐ | Hot Cocoa | `dungeon.hot-chocolate` | group | A pot of cocoa, still warm, and a note: FOR WHOEVER'S NEXT. Everyone sips, slowly. |  |
| ☐ | The Shortcut | `dungeon.two-shelves` | group | A teleport circle chalked on the floor, half rubbed out. It might go up. |  |
| ☐ | The Mirrors | `dungeon.the-meat-hooks` | group | Walk past the dark mirrors, or step through the one that's waving at you. |  |
| ☐ | The Wizard Walk | `dungeon.freezer-penguin-walk` | group | The drawer walks like a very old wizard to the nearest wall and back. Refuse: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Prophecy | `dungeon.freezer-cold-take` | group | Everyone makes a prophecy about their left neighbor. The table picks the best; they give 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Ritual | `dungeon.freezer-huddle` | group | Everyone holds hands for the ritual. Anyone who won't drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | Spellcraft | `dungeon.freezer-hot-cocoa` | group | Everyone wiggles their fingers like a wizard casting, then takes a slow sip. |  |
| ☐ | Fireball Fight | `dungeon.freezer-cold-takes` | group | On three, everyone throws a mime fireball at someone. Whoever is hit most drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | The View | `dungeon.the-view` | group | The whole realm, laid out below. Everyone stops, looks, and drinks to it. |  |
| ☐ | The Fire Pit | `dungeon.the-fire-pit` | group | A fire pit and a ring of rocks. Everyone sits closer than they need to and sips. |  |
| ☐ | A Cry for Help | `dungeon.the-two-terraces` | group | A horn from a stuck caravan up the pass: three short blasts, for help. |  |
| ☐ | The Ice Cave | `dungeon.the-fire-escape` | group | Climb over the ridge, or crawl through the ice cave, where someone is shouting. |  |
| ☐ | Peak Toast | `dungeon.rooftop-skyline-toast` | group | Everyone toasts something they can see from up here. Repeat a toast: drink 2. |  |
| ☐ | Shooting Star | `dungeon.rooftop-shooting-star` | group | The drawer makes a wish out loud. If the table thinks it's lame, the drawer drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | Group Portrait | `dungeon.rooftop-pigeon` | group | Everyone squeezes into one pose for the painter. Anyone cut off or blinking drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | Fresh Air | `dungeon.rooftop-fresh-air` | group | Everyone takes a deep breath of fresh air. Then a sip. |  |
| ☐ | First Down | `dungeon.rooftop-first-to-post` | group | On three, everyone points at who'll be first down the mountain. Most fingers: give 3. |  |
| ☐ | The King's Decree | `dungeon.summons-proclamation` | group | {dragon} has woken under {lair}. Everyone raises a glass to the king. |  |
| ☐ | A Burned Village | `dungeon.summons-burned-village` | group | A village in ashes; {dragon} flew home to {lair}. Everyone drinks 1 for it. |  |
| ☐ | The Bounty | `dungeon.summons-wanted-poster` | group | WANTED: {dragon}. LAST SEEN: {lair}. Everyone toasts the reward. |  |
| ☐ | The Prophecy | `dungeon.summons-prophecy` | group | A fool will slay {dragon}. Everyone points at the fool; most fingers drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | A Princess Writes | `dungeon.summons-princess` | group | '{dragon} took me to {lair}. Bring cake.' The drawer reads it aloud, sobbing. |  |
| ☐ | The Last Survivor | `dungeon.summons-survivor` | group | A singed knight gasps '{dragon}... {lair}...' and faints. Everyone toasts him. |  |
| ☐ | The Shadow | `dungeon.sign-shadow` | group | A shadow crosses the sun: {dragon}. Everyone ducks. Last head down drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | A Fallen Scale | `dungeon.sign-scale` | group | A scale the size of a shield, still warm, fallen from {dragon}. Everyone touches it. |  |
| ☐ | The Fleeing Knights | `dungeon.sign-knights` | group | Knights gallop past the wrong way, shouting '{dragon}!' Everyone shouts it too. |  |
| ☐ | The Burned Mill | `dungeon.sign-mill` | group | A mill, still smoking. {dragon} was here an hour ago. Everyone pours out a sip. |  |
| ☐ | Tracks | `dungeon.sign-tracks` | group | One footprint, big enough to sit in. Everyone sits in it. Last down drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | The Old Song | `dungeon.sign-song` | group | A shepherd sings the old song about {dragon}. Everyone hums along. Nobody knows it. |  |
| ☐ | The Road Out | `dungeon.road-out-parade` | group | The King's Road is closed for a parade. Forest or marsh? Everyone votes; the drawer decides. |  |
| ☐ | The Road Out | `dungeon.road-out-elves` | group | The forest is shut: the elves are sulking. Road or marsh? Everyone votes; the drawer decides. |  |
| ☐ | The Road Out | `dungeon.road-out-flood` | group | The marsh is flooded to the eaves. Road or forest? Everyone votes; the drawer decides. |  |
| ☐ | The Signpost | `dungeon.road-sign-full` | group | The crypt is full. Mines or tower? Everyone votes; the drawer decides. |  |
| ☐ | The Signpost | `dungeon.road-sign-strike` | group | The mines are on strike. Crypt or tower? Everyone votes; the drawer decides. |  |
| ☐ | The Signpost | `dungeon.road-sign-moved` | group | The tower has moved again. Crypt or mines? Everyone votes; the drawer decides. |  |
| ☐ | The Gallows Tree | `dungeon.landmark-gallows-tree` | group | A man on the gallows swears he's innocent. Everyone votes: guilty or not. |  |
| ☐ | The Tourney Field | `dungeon.landmark-tourney-field` | group | A joust, and they need one more fool. Everyone points at the drawer. |  |
| ☐ | The Toll Keep | `dungeon.landmark-toll-keep` | group | A baron's keep across the road, and the toll is due. The drawer counts their coins aloud. |  |
| ☐ | The Fairy Court | `dungeon.landmark-fairy-court` | group | The fair folk offer a gift and never say the price. Everyone bows to the queen. |  |
| ☐ | The Oldest Tree | `dungeon.landmark-oldest-tree` | group | It has watched every hero pass, and it has notes. The drawer reads them aloud. |  |
| ☐ | The Hunter's Feast | `dungeon.landmark-hunters-feast` | group | A lord's feast in a clearing, and you were not invited. Everyone grabs a bite. |  |
| ☐ | The Sunken Chapel | `dungeon.landmark-sunken-chapel` | group | A chapel up to its windows in mud, and the bell still rings. Everyone says 'ding'. |  |
| ☐ | The Hag's Bargain | `dungeon.landmark-hags-bargain` | group | She'll trade something you have for something you need. The drawer shakes her clammy hand. |  |
| ☐ | The Ferry of Frogs | `dungeon.landmark-frog-ferry` | group | A raft poled by frogs, and the fare is a song. Everyone croaks the chorus. |  |
| ☐ | The Dead Ferryman | `dungeon.landmark-dead-ferryman` | group | He'll row you past the worst of the crypt. Everyone closes their eyes for the crossing. | Jinx The Ferryman's Due: The cursed player says 'for the ferryman' before every drink. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Hall of Kings | `dungeon.landmark-hall-of-kings` | group | Twelve dead kings on twelve thrones, one empty. The drawer stares at it a little too long. |  |
| ☐ | The Weeping Statue | `dungeon.landmark-weeping-statue` | group | A stone queen weeps real tears, and they heal. Everyone keeps a straight face. | Jinx Stone Tears: The cursed player sobs loudly before every drink. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Goblin Market | `dungeon.landmark-goblin-market` | group | Everything is for sale, and most of it is yours. The drawer checks their pockets. | Jinx Goblin Grudge: The cursed player keeps one hand over their glass. Slip: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Lift | `dungeon.landmark-the-lift` | group | A cage on a rusty chain, or a thousand stairs down. Everyone listens to it creak. |  |
| ☐ | The Dwarf Ghosts | `dungeon.landmark-dwarf-ghosts` | group | The old miners want one last shift. Everyone swings an invisible pick on three. | Jinx Haunted Shift: The cursed player whistles a mining song before every drink. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Room of Doors | `dungeon.landmark-room-of-doors` | group | Three doors: one hums, one smokes, one is very polite. Everyone picks one aloud. |  |
| ☐ | The Library | `dungeon.landmark-the-library` | group | Every book ever written, all badly shelved. The drawer names the last book they read. | Jinx Overdue: The cursed player shushes anyone who talks over them. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Familiar's Exam | `dungeon.landmark-familiars-exam` | group | The wizard's cat tests you before you may pass. Everyone meows on three. |  |
| ☐ | The Bone Road | `dungeon.landmark-bone-road` | group | Empty armor all the way down to {dragon}. Everyone salutes the ones who came first. |  |
| ☐ | The Last Forge | `dungeon.landmark-last-forge` | group | A dwarf smith forges for whoever faces {dragon}. Everyone hammers the table. |  |
| ☐ | The Hoard's Edge | `dungeon.landmark-hoards-edge` | group | Gold underfoot already, and all of it {dragon}'s. Everyone tiptoes in silence. | Jinx Dragon Gold: The cursed player jingles their pockets before every drink. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Frozen Army | `dungeon.landmark-frozen-army` | group | A hundred knights in ice, swords raised at {dragon}. Everyone freezes for three seconds. |  |
| ☐ | The Wind Shrine | `dungeon.landmark-wind-shrine` | group | Prayer flags, and an old monk who has seen {dragon}. Everyone bows to the wind. |  |
| ☐ | The Glass Bridge | `dungeon.landmark-glass-bridge` | group | A bridge of clear ice, or the long way round. Everyone holds their breath to cross. |  |
| ☐ | The Bell Reef | `dungeon.landmark-bell-reef` | group | Ships' bells hung on the reef by {dragon}. Everyone rings one: 'ding'. |  |
| ☐ | The Drowned Town | `dungeon.landmark-drowned-town` | group | Roofs under clear water, and a lamp still lit. Everyone holds their breath to look. |  |
| ☐ | The Last Lighthouse | `dungeon.landmark-last-lighthouse` | group | The keeper is leaving before {dragon} comes back. Everyone waves goodbye to him. |  |
| ☐ | The Sleeping Goat | `dungeon.landing-stairwell` | group | A fat cat on every chair. Everyone gives the cat on their left a noble name. |  |
| ☐ | The Crooked Lantern | `dungeon.landing-smoking-area` | group | A crooked inn with a straight fire. Everyone names their best moment of the quest so far. |  |
| ☐ | A Hayloft | `dungeon.landing-coat-check` | group | A barn's hayloft, warm and itchy. Everyone names the animal they'd ride into battle. |  |
| ☐ | The Ferry House | `dungeon.landing-bathroom-line` | group | The ferryman's inn, and everyone's soaked. Everyone hypes their left neighbor like a herald. |  |
| ☐ | A Shepherd's Hut | `dungeon.landing-loading-dock` | group | A shepherd shares his bread and his opinions. Everyone names a snack worth fighting for. |  |
| ☐ | The Pilgrims' Rest | `dungeon.landing-phone-booth` | group | A shrine by the door and a bell that rings itself. Everyone says one thing they'd pray for. |  |
| ☐ | The Drunken Dwarf | `dungeon.landing-mop-closet` | group | The dwarf who runs it arm-wrestles every guest. Everyone gives their left neighbor a dwarf name. |  |
| ☐ | A Painted Wagon | `dungeon.landing-photo-booth` | group | Traveling players let you sleep in their wagon. Everyone strikes a heroic pose on three. |  |
| ☐ | The Last Warm Fire | `dungeon.landing-boiler-room` | group | Past here, only {dragon}. Everyone warms their hands and names one thing they'll miss. |  |
| ☐ | A Campfire | `dungeon.landing-campfire` | group | A fire under the stars. Everyone tells the scariest thing they've heard about {dragon}. |  |
| ☐ | The Monastery | `dungeon.landing-staff-room` | group | Monks who pray against {dragon}. Everyone stays silent a minute. First sound drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | The Watchtower | `dungeon.landing-fire-escape` | group | A ruined tower; far off, smoke from {dragon}. Everyone toasts how far you've come. |  |
| ☐ | The Hermit's Cave | `dungeon.landing-service-stairs` | group | A hermit who once met {dragon}. Everyone tells him one piece of gossip. |  |
| ☐ | The Empty Fort | `dungeon.landing-the-bunker` | group | A fort abandoned when {dragon} woke. Everyone names who they'd want on its walls. |  |
| ☐ | The Hot Springs | `dungeon.landing-laundry-room` | group | Springs warm as {dragon}'s breath. Everyone confesses one secret to the party. |  |
| ☐ | The Haunted Inn | `dungeon.landing-stuck-elevator` | group | The innkeeper is a ghost; {dragon} got him. Everyone hums his song on three. |  |
| ☐ | The Miller's Plea | `dungeon.quest-goat` | group | A miller begs: a witch took his goat, and the goat is his best friend. Everyone says 'baa' once. |  |
| ☐ | The Merchant's Plea | `dungeon.quest-strongbox` | group | A merchant, robbed blind, wants his strongbox back. Everyone checks their own pockets, loudly. |  |
| ☐ | The Gravedigger's Plea | `dungeon.quest-barrow` | group | The gravedigger says the dead won't stay down. Everyone shows their best zombie face on three. |  |
| ☐ | The Fisherman's Plea | `dungeon.quest-nets` | group | A fisherman says the old shrine sings at night and his nets come up empty. Everyone hums low. |  |
| ☐ | The Dwarf's Plea | `dungeon.quest-vault` | group | A dwarf says something big took his family's vault. Everyone strokes an imaginary beard. |  |
| ☐ | Highwaymen | `dungeon.gelatinous-shot` | challenge | Three masked riders block the road. Your money or your life. Hands up, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! They take the money and your boots. Drink 6, hands still up. 2-6: They take the money. Drink 3, hands still up. 7-19: You take their horses. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You take their horses and their masks. Give 6. |
| ☐ | The Toll Gate | `dungeon.rat-trench-coat` | challenge | A gate guard wants papers. Left neighbor plays him: talk your way past in 10 seconds, or drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Wolf Pack | `dungeon.mold-elemental` | challenge | Eyes in the trees, then teeth. Howl at them, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! They drag you off the road. Drink 6, then howl at your left neighbor. 2-6: They circle and bite. Drink 3, and howl once more. 7-19: They scatter into the trees. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! The alpha rolls over. Everyone else howls; the quietest drinks 6. |
| ☐ | Goblin Scouts | `dungeon.drunk-goblin` | challenge | Two goblins on one stolen pony, both steering. Hold out your reins, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! The pony kicks you twice. Drink 6, then neigh at your right neighbor. 2-6: The pony runs you over. Drink 3 and neigh. 7-19: They fall off. Loot their saddlebags. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! They fall off, and the pony likes you better. Give 4, and everyone neighs. |
| ☐ | Wanted Poster | `dungeon.skeleton-barback` | challenge | A wanted poster on a tree looks like one of you. On three, everyone points. Most fingers: drink 2. |  |
| ☐ | Snake in the Road | `dungeon.possessed-mop` | challenge | A snake crosses the road. Last to lift both feet off the floor drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | Giant Spider | `dungeon.keg-spider` | challenge | It hangs over the road, waiting. Wiggle eight fingers at it, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Wrapped up for later. Drink 6, then spider-walk to the door and back. 2-6: Bitten. Drink 3, then spider-walk to the door and back. 7-19: Squished under a cartwheel. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! Squished, and its web held a purse of gold. Give 6. |
| ☐ | The Tax Collector | `dungeon.tab-goblin` | challenge | The tax collector wants his cut. Right neighbor plays him: thumb war, best of three. Lose: drink 4. |  |
| ☐ | Hedge Witch | `dungeon.goblin-shaman` | challenge | A woman in a hat of twigs is cursing travelers for fun. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! The curse lands. Drink 6. 2-6: The curse lands. Drink 3. 7-19: You knock her hat off. Give 3 to whoever looks most cursed. 20-20: Natural 20! You knock her hat off. Give 6 to whoever looks most cursed. · Jinx Toad Voice: The cursed player croaks after every sentence. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | Bats Under the Bridge | `dungeon.cask-bat` | challenge | Light the fuse. Pass a cup clockwise, naming a weapon as you pass. The bats hit whoever holds it. | Fuse 12–40 s: The bats swoop. Whoever is holding the cup drinks 3. |
| ☐ | The Rope Bridge | `dungeon.clipboard-troll` | challenge | A rope bridge, one plank wide. Walk heel to toe across the room and back. Wobble: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Sphinx | `dungeon.riddling-tap` | challenge | A sphinx sits across the road. Your left neighbor asks a riddle: 10 seconds, or drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Stuck Cart | `dungeon.stuck-door` | challenge | A cart is stuck in the mud. Push it: wall-sit for 30 seconds. Stand early: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Stare-Down | `dungeon.keg-stand-off` | challenge | A knight blocks the road and stares. Right neighbor plays him. Blink or laugh first: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Mud Pit | `dungeon.sticky-floor` | challenge | The road turns to knee-deep mud. Roll d20 to pull free. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You leave a boot behind. Drink 4, and stay one shoe down until your next turn. 2-8: You leave a boot behind. Drink 2, and stay one shoe down until your next turn. 9-19: You pull free. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You pull free and moonwalk out. Everyone copies it; the worst moonwalk drinks 4. |
| ☐ | Tripwire | `dungeon.christmas-lights` | challenge | Bandits strung a wire across the road at shin height. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You eat the road. Drink 4. 2-8: You eat the road. Drink 2. 9-19: You hop it. Give 2 to the clumsiest player here. 20-20: Natural 20! You hop it. Give 4 to the clumsiest player here. |
| ☐ | Runaway Cart | `dungeon.rolling-kegs` | challenge | A cart full of cabbages comes down the hill. Nobody is driving it. Roll d20 to dive. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Flattened. Drink 6, lying down if you can. 2-8: Flattened. Drink 3, lying down if you can. 9-19: You dive clear. Give 2 to whoever didn't flinch. 20-20: Natural 20! You dive clear. Give 4 to whoever didn't flinch. |
| ☐ | Falling Acorns | `dungeon.falling-taps` | challenge | An acorn bonks you on the head, and you're Thumb Master now. Drink 1, then use it. | Jinx Thumb Master: Whenever the cursed player puts a thumb on the table, the last to copy drinks 1. |
| ☐ | Lost Pilgrims | `dungeon.lost-bridesmaids` | challenge | A line of pilgrims has decided you're the prophet. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You lead the hymn. Drink 4, then sway with your right neighbor. 2-6: You lead the hymn. Drink 2, then sway with your right neighbor. 7-19: You point them to the next town. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You point them to the next town. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Wandering Minstrels | `dungeon.rat-pack` | challenge | Three minstrels sing at you for coin. Sing them a line back, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! They follow you for a mile. Drink 4. 2-6: They follow you for a mile. Drink 2. 7-19: You out-sing them. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You out-sing them. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Stampede | `dungeon.fryer-oil-ooze` | challenge | A herd of cattle comes down the road, and it runs clockwise from you. Roll four dice. | 4d6 — It tramples four seats. You drink {first}; the next three clockwise drink {second}, {third} and {fourth}. |
| ☐ | Pickpocket | `dungeon.barback-poltergeist` | challenge | A small boy keeps bumping into you. Your purse is lighter every time. Roll 2d6. | 2d6 — 2-5: He's gone, and so is your purse. Drink 3. 6-12: You catch him by the collar. Make someone turn out their pockets, then give 3. |
| ☐ | The Low Branch | `dungeon.low-beam` | challenge | Your right neighbor holds out an arm as the branch. Limbo under it. Touch it: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Rotten Plank | `dungeon.loose-floorboard` | challenge | One plank on the bridge is not like the others. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It snaps under you. Drink 4. 2-8: It snaps under you. Drink 2. 9-19: You step over it like a cat. Meow, then give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You step over it like a cat. Meow, then give 4. |
| ☐ | The Wedding Party | `dungeon.stag-party` | challenge | A whole wedding party on the road, and they're one guest short. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Invited. Drink 4, then give a toast to the couple. 2-6: Invited. Drink 2, then give a toast to the couple. 7-19: You send them the long way round. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You send them the long way round. Give 4. |
| ☐ | The Black Knight | `dungeon.grease-trap-horror` | challenge | He has held this crossing for years and lets no one pass. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Knocked flat. Drink 8, then bow to him. 2-9: Knocked flat. Drink 4, then bow to him. 10-19: He yields, finally. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! He yields, finally. Give 8. |
| ☐ | The Sheriff | `dungeon.health-inspector` | challenge | The sheriff says you match a description. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! The stocks. Drink 8 and name three things you didn't do. 2-9: The stocks. Drink 4 and name three things you didn't do. 10-19: You bribe him, and he drops his badge. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You bribe him, and he drops his badge. Give 8. |
| ☐ | The Bandit Queen | `dungeon.keg-mimic-queen` | challenge | Every bandit in the woods answers to her, and they're all here. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Robbed blind. Drink 8. 2-9: Robbed blind. Drink 4. 10-19: You take her crown, and her gang scatters. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You take her crown, and her gang scatters. Give 8. |
| ☐ | The Witch Hunter | `dungeon.the-exterminator` | challenge | He hunts witches, and he has decided you count. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Dunked in the river. Drink 8, holding your breath. 2-9: Dunked in the river. Drink 4, holding your breath. 10-19: You convince him he's the witch. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You convince him he's the witch. Give 8. |
| ☐ | The Bridge Troll | `dungeon.keg-golem` | challenge | He wants a toll. Side bets: thumbs up or down on you; wrong bets drink 1. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He takes the toll out of you. Drink 8. 2-8: He takes the toll out of you. Drink 4. 9-19: He falls off his own bridge. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! He falls off, and pays you a toll. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: He climbs back up, dripping. Arm-wrestle your left neighbor, playing the troll. Lose: drink 3. (Won/Lost) |
| ☐ | The Robber Baron | `dungeon.rat-king` | challenge | He says he owns this road, and everyone on it. Kneel, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! His court finds you guilty, and broke. Finish your drink. 2-8: His court finds you guilty. Drink 4. 9-19: You take his keep. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You take his keep. Give 10. · Beat 2: His court is in session. Plead your case to the table for 20 seconds; they vote. Guilty: drink 3. (Won/Lost) · Jinx My Lord: Everyone calls the cursed player My Lord. Slip: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Ogre | `dungeon.the-landlord` | challenge | An ogre blocks the road. Side bets: thumbs up or down on you; wrong bets drink 1. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He eats your lunch, then you. Finish your drink. 2-8: He takes a bite. Drink 4. 9-19: You trick him into a sack. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You trick him into a sack. Give 10. · Beat 2: He gets up and swings a tree at you. Roll 2d6. (2d6 — 2-6: The tree connects. Drink 4. 7-12: You duck it. Give 4.) · Jinx Ogre Talk: The cursed player speaks only in one-word grunts. Uses two words: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Great Boar | `dungeon.big-cheese` | challenge | A cart-sized boar. Side bets: thumbs up or down on you; wrong bets drink 1. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Gored. Drink 8. 2-8: Gored. Drink 4. 9-19: You bring it down. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You bring it down, and dinner is served. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: It turns and charges again, tusks first. Roll d6. (1d6 — 1-1: Trampled. Drink 4. 2-2: Tusked. Drink 3. 3-3: You dodge. Give 3. 4-4: You grab a tusk and hang on. Give 3. 5-5: It runs into a tree. Give 4. 6-6: You ride it. Everyone else drinks 2.) |
| ☐ | The Headless Rider | `dungeon.the-pipe-organ` | challenge | A rider with no head, galloping right at you. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He takes your head for a while. Shut your eyes for 10 seconds, then drink 8. 2-8: He swings low. Drink 4. 9-19: You knock him out of the saddle. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You knock him out of the saddle. Give 10. · Beat 2: He rides back. Left neighbor plays him: rock, paper, scissors, best of three. Lose: drink 3. (Won/Lost) · Jinx Spooked: The cursed player screams whenever anyone says 'head'. Misses one: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Talking Oak | `dungeon.boxed-wine-golem` | challenge | An old oak asks why you're in its forest. Give it a reason, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It isn't convinced, and drops a branch on you. Drink 6. 2-6: It isn't convinced, and drops a branch on you. Drink 3. 7-19: It laughs, and the branches part. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! It laughs so hard it drops every acorn it has. Give 6. |
| ☐ | Pixie Swarm | `dungeon.cork-imp` | challenge | A swarm of pixies, giggling and pulling your hair. Roll d6. | 1d6 — 1-1: One gets in your ear. Drink 3, head tilted. 2-2: They tie your laces together. Drink 3. 3-3: You swat one. Give 2. 4-4: You trap them under a hat. Give 3. 5-5: You out-giggle them. Give 3. 6-6: They adopt you. Everyone else drinks 1. |
| ☐ | The Silent Druid | `dungeon.zombie-sommelier` | challenge | Read alone what the druid is thinking, start the clock, then act it out. The table guesses. | Secret: A BEAR IN A HURRY. · Clock 45 s: Time. Someone guessed it? Give 3. Nobody did? Drink 3. |
| ☐ | The Howling Hag | `dungeon.wine-mom-banshee` | challenge | Howl at the moon against your left neighbor. The table calls the quieter: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Fairy Ring | `dungeon.rose-slime` | challenge | A ring of red mushrooms, and you're standing in it. Dance a little, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You dance until dawn. Drink 6. 2-6: The fairies make you keep dancing. Drink 3. 7-19: You dance out of the ring. A puff of spores follows you. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You dance out of the ring. A puff of spores follows you. Give 4. |
| ☐ | The Wisp | `dungeon.decanter-ghost` | challenge | A little light in the trees, bobbing, leading you off the path. Roll 2d6. | 2d6 — 2-5: You follow it into a bog. Drink 3, and say 'which way?' 6-12: You blow it out like a candle. Give 3 to whoever looks most lost. · Jinx Wisp-Struck: Nobody may look the cursed player in the eye. Caught: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Hollow Log | `dungeon.barrel-mimic` | challenge | On three, everyone points at who'd hide in a hollow log first. Most fingers: drink 2. |  |
| ☐ | Pinecones! | `dungeon.corkscrew-crab` | challenge | A pinecone storm in the canopy. Everyone ducks on three; the last to duck drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | The Acorn Sprite | `dungeon.sangria-sprite` | challenge | Your right neighbor throws a mimed acorn. Catch it in your mouth. Booed: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Bramble Witch | `dungeon.vinegar-vampire` | challenge | A witch made of thorns, sure you trampled her garden. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You did trample it. Drink 6, and say sorry to a plant. 2-6: You did trample it. Drink 3, and say sorry to a plant. 7-19: You compliment her roses. She blushes thorns. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You compliment her roses. She blushes thorns. Give 6. · Jinx Thorny: The cursed player says 'ouch' before every sip. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Elves' Test | `dungeon.blind-tasting` | challenge | Eyes shut; the elves hand you someone's drink. Name whose it is. Wrong: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Heron | `dungeon.coaster-nose` | challenge | Stand on one leg like a heron, eyes shut, for 10 seconds. Wobble or peek: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Elf Lord's Wine | `dungeon.swirl-sniff-lie` | challenge | Review your drink like a very old elf lord for 15 seconds. Crack a smile: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Ode to an Oak | `dungeon.the-toast` | challenge | Praise your right neighbor like an ancient tree for 20 seconds. Anyone laughs: you drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Falling Branches | `dungeon.falling-rack` | challenge | A storm in the canopy, and the branches start coming down. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Bonked. Drink 6. 2-8: Bonked. Drink 3. 9-19: You catch the last one. Give 2, and hand someone your 'branch'. 20-20: Natural 20! You catch the last one. Give 4, and hand someone your 'branch'. |
| ☐ | The Mossy Slope | `dungeon.merlot-stairs` | challenge | A slope of wet moss, and the path is at the bottom. Stand up, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Down on your backside, in green. Drink 4 standing up. 2-8: Down on your backside, in green. Drink 2 standing up. 9-19: Not a smudge on you. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You slide down it like a sled. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Snares | `dungeon.cork-mines` | challenge | Hunters' snares all over the path. Some of them twitch. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Snap. Hung up by one ankle. Drink 4. 2-8: Snap. Hung up by one ankle. Drink 2. 9-19: You tiptoe through. Give 2 to whoever walks loudest. 20-20: Natural 20! You tiptoe through. Give 4 to whoever walks loudest. |
| ☐ | Midge Cloud | `dungeon.fruit-fly-swarm` | challenge | A cloud of midges off the pond, and they brought friends. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! In your mouth. Drink 4. 2-6: They pat you on the head. Drink 2. 7-19: They're charmed and wave you on. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! They're charmed and wave you on. Give 4. |
| ☐ | The Woodpecker | `dungeon.cork-goblin` | challenge | A woodpecker goes to work on the tree beside you. Everyone does a woodpecker; the worst drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | The Green Man | `dungeon.rose-elemental` | challenge | Everyone says 'the forest remembers' as a threat. The table picks the scariest; they give 3. |  |

| ☐ | The Sinkhole | `dungeon.sediment-pit` | challenge | Leaves over a hole, and the hole is full of mud. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You wade through. Drink 4, and squelch. 2-8: You wade through. Drink 2, and squelch. 9-19: You vault it. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You vault it. Give 4. |

Give 8. |

| ☐ | The Woodcutter | `dungeon.cellar-master` | challenge | He knows every tree in this forest, and you broke one. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He makes you stack wood. | ☐ | The Faerie Bard | `dungeon.wine-influencer` | challenge | A faerie bard who needs a villain for her new song, and has picked you. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You hum over her until she gives up. Give 8. |
| ☐ | The Wolf King | `dungeon.count-corkula` | challenge | He means to eat you first. Side bets: thumbs up or down; wrong bets drink 1. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He takes a bite. Drink 8. 2-8: He takes a bite. Drink 4. 9-19: You knock out a fang. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! He runs off yelping. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: He circles you. Staring contest with your left neighbor, playing the wolf. Blink first: drink 3. (Won/Lost) · Jinx Howler: The cursed player howls after every sip. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Faerie Queen | `dungeon.cask-queen` | challenge | She sits on a throne of thorns and judges your manners. Curtsy, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Off with your head, briefly. Finish your drink. 2-8: Your manners are basic. Drink 4. 9-19: You cut down her throne, and she tumbles. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You cut down her throne, and she tumbles. Give 10. · Beat 2: She demands a toast. Give her one, ten seconds, no ums; the table judges. Fail: drink 3. (Won/Lost) · Jinx Curtsy Court: The cursed player curtsies before every drink. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Old Bear | `dungeon.chateau-ghoul` | challenge | A bear older than the forest, woken from a very long nap. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He sits on you. Drink 8. 2-8: He sits on you. Roll 2d6 to prove you have none. (2d6 — 2-6: You have honey on you. Drink 4. 7-12: Empty-handed. Give 4.) |
| ☐ | The Green Knight | `dungeon.the-sommelier` | challenge | Twelve feet tall and green as moss. Side bets: thumbs up or down; wrong bets drink 1. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He takes your head and keeps it a while. Drink 8. 2-8: He takes your head, then gives it back. Drink 4. 9-19: You land a blow he'll remember. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You land a blow he'll remember. Give 10. · Beat 2: His game: you strike, then he does. Arm-wrestle your left neighbor, playing him. Lose: drink 3. (Won/Lost) |
| ☐ | The Hungry Wood | `dungeon.the-barrel-room` | challenge | The trees close in, and one of them has a mouth. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Swallowed by roots. Drink 8. 2-8: Swallowed by roots. Drink 4. 9-19: You find the tree with the mouth and kick it. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You turn the other trees against it. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: The roots grab for you. Roll d6. (1d6 — 1-1: They drag you under. Drink 4. 2-2: They grab your foot. Drink 3. 3-3: You jump them. Give 3. 4-4: You stamp on one. Give 3. 5-5: They tangle each other up. Give 4. 6-6: You ride a root out. Everyone else drinks 2.) · Jinx Root-Bound: The cursed player keeps both feet flat on the floor. Lifts one: drink 1. |

You lay them to rest with a song. Give 8. |

You read it a longer list. Give 8 to whoever holds the oldest grudge. |

You sip, and ask for seconds. Drink 6 and say it was lovely. 2-8: You sip. Drink 3 and say it tasted fine. 9-19: You pour it back. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You pour it back. Give 8. · Jinx Fumes: The cursed player can only whisper. Too loud: drink 1. |
| ☐ | Ooze Pup | `dungeon.fatberg-spawn` | challenge | A baby ooze made of mine slime. It wants its mother. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It clings. Drink 6. 2-8: It clings. Drink 3. 9-19: You scrape it off. Give 4, and make the noise. 20-20: Natural 20! You scrape it off. Give 8, and make the noise. · Jinx Slimy Fingers: The cursed player holds their drink with two fingers only. Caught: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Four-Armed Ogre | `dungeon.sewer-mutant` | challenge | The four-armed ogre arm-wrestles the table, clockwise. Roll four dice. | 4d6 — Four arms pinned. You drink {first}; the next three clockwise drink {second}, {third} and {fourth}. |
| ☐ | The Old Dwarf | `dungeon.turtle-sensei` | challenge | A dwarf who trained four heroes and got nothing for it. He wants a fight. Roll d6. | 1d6 — 1-1: Hammered. Drink 4. 2-2: He lectures you on gratitude. Drink 4. 3-3: You bow first. He respects it. Give 3. 4-4: You flip him over his anvil. Give 4. 5-5: You thank him. He cries. Give 4. 6-6: You do a perfect axe spin. Everyone else drinks 2. |
| ☐ | The Echo Cave | `dungeon.echo-bat` | challenge | Your right neighbor echoes everything you say for 20 seconds. You laugh first: drink 4. | Jinx Bat Signal: Whenever the cursed player points at the ceiling, the last to copy drinks 1. |

| ☐ | Spell the Mine | `dungeon.the-echo` | challenge | Say a word starting with each letter of GOBLIN, in order, in 10 seconds. Stumble: drink 4. |  |

Drink 3, and pat out your hair. 11-19: You dive into a puddle. Give 2 to whoever you blame. 20-20: Natural 20! You dive into a puddle. Give 4 to whoever you blame. |

Drink 6. 2-10: Down the chute, face first. Drink 3. 11-19: You surf it. Give 2 to the worst surfer here. 20-20: Natural 20! You surf it. Give 4 to the worst surfer here. |

| ☐ | The Goblin King | `dungeon.the-fatberg` | challenge | A goblin king on a throne of pans. Side bets: thumbs up or down; wrong bets drink 1. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He has you pelted with spoons. Drink 10. 2-11: He has you pelted with spoons. Drink 5. 12-19: You knock over his throne. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You steal his crown. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: His goblins rush back in. Roll 2d6. (2d6 — 2-7: They pile on you. Drink 5. 8-12: You scatter them. Give 4.) · Jinx The Royal We: The cursed player can't say 'I' or 'me'. Slip: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Ogre Foreman | `dungeon.alligator-al` | challenge | An ogre with a whip and a quota. Side bets: thumbs up or down; wrong bets drink 1. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: He wants your miner's stance. Hold it on one leg while the table counts to 15. Wobble: drink 4. (Won/Lost) |
| ☐ | The Mine Lord | `dungeon.plumber-king` | challenge | A dwarf lord crowned in gold, mad with greed, sure you're a thief. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He crowns you, and now you're mad too. Finish your drink. 2-11: He hits you with a gold bar. Drink 5. 12-19: You snap his scepter. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You snap his scepter. Give 8. · Beat 2: Kneel and swear fealty to the Mine Lord, in one breath, in rhyme. Stumble: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Jinx No Pointing: Nobody points at anything. Slip: drink 1. |
| ☐ | Mother of Goblins | `dungeon.the-crocodile-mom` | challenge | The goblins' mother has heard what you did to her boys. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! She adopts you. | ☐ | The Rock Worm | `dungeon.the-pipe-worm` | challenge | Eighty years in the tunnels, and now it's as long as the mountain. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It wraps around you twice. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: It comes up through the floor behind you. Roll d6. (1d6 — 1-1: It swallows your arm. Drink 5. 2-2: It swallows your drink. Drink 4. 3-3: It hisses. Slip: drink 1. |

| ☐ | The Mad Alchemist | `dungeon.the-snow-bartender` | challenge | He brews only exploding potions, and he never stops pouring. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You ask for seconds. Give 10. · Beat 2: He serves the house special. Roll 2d6. (2d6 — 2-7: It explodes. Drink 5. 8-12: You sip it slowly, like an adult. Give 5.) · Jinx Bubbles: The cursed player ends every sentence with 'bloop'. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Archmage | `dungeon.absolute-zero` | challenge | The oldest wizard alive. He doesn't move, and nothing near him does either. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You stop moving. Drink 10. 2-11: You stop moving. Drink 5. 12-19: You sneeze, and break his focus. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You set his beard on fire. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: He casts a holding spell. Freeze while the table counts to 15. Move, laugh or blink: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Jinx Countdown: The cursed player counts down from three, out loud, before every sip. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Puppet Master | `dungeon.the-ice-cream-man` | challenge | A wizard with strings on every finger. Side bets: thumbs up or down; wrong bets drink 1. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You become a puppet. Finish your drink. 2-11: You dance for him. Drink 5. 12-19: You cut a string. He can't cope. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You cut a string. He can't cope. Give 10. · Beat 2: He makes the table dance. Name six dances in ten seconds. Fall short: drink 4. (Won/Lost) |
| ☐ | The Frozen Apprentice | `dungeon.the-frozen-ex` | challenge | The wizard's last apprentice, frozen mid-spell a hundred years ago, now thawing. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He finishes the spell, on you. Drink 10. 2-11: He finishes the spell, on you. Drink 5. 12-19: You tell him the wizard retired. He shatters. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! He thanks you and melts, politely. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: He asks what he missed. Name three new things in ten seconds. Fall short: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Jinx As I Was Saying: The cursed player starts every sentence with 'As I was saying'. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Eagle King | `dungeon.pigeon-king` | challenge | Screech-off with your left neighbor; the table picks the better eagle. Worse eagle: drink 5. |  |
| ☐ | The Mountain Horn | `dungeon.rooftop-dj` | challenge | A giant on the peak blows a horn, and he takes requests personally. Roll d6. | 1d6 — 1-1: He blows your name into the echo. Drink 5. 2-2: He plays your ex's song. Drink 5. 3-3: The horn, right in your ear. Drink 5. 4-4: You request a war song. Give 4. 5-5: You plug the horn with snow. Give 5. 6-6: You take the horn. Everyone else drinks 2. |
| ☐ | The Wind | `dungeon.the-wind` | challenge | Not a monster. Just the wind, a mile up, and it wants your drink. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It takes your hat and your dignity. Drink 10. 2-11: It takes your hat and your dignity. Drink 5. 12-19: You lean into it like a hero on a cliff. Hair flip, then give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You lean into it like a hero on a cliff. Hair flip, then give 10. · Jinx Windswept: The cursed player flips their hair before speaking. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Snow Owl | `dungeon.the-seagull` | challenge | A snow owl eyes the table. Last to cover their drink with a hand drinks 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Ice Golem | `dungeon.influencer-golem` | challenge | A golem of ice blocks needs one more snowman, and you're the head. Roll 2d6. | 2d6 — 2-7: Packed into a snowman. Drink 5. 8-12: You knock its head off. Give 5 to whoever's posing. |
| ☐ | The Hermit | `dungeon.the-neighbor` | challenge | A hermit on the next ledge threatens to start an avalanche. Wave at him, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He shouts, and it's your mom. Drink 10, and wave again. 2-11: He shouts. Drink 5. 12-19: You invite him over. Pick someone to be the hermit; they drink 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You invite him over. Pick someone to be the hermit; they drink 10. · Jinx Avalanche Rules: The cursed player may only whisper. Speak up: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Ice Troll | `dungeon.the-gargoyle` | challenge | An ice troll on the ledge, pretending to be a rock. Everyone freezes while you roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It drops on you. Drink 10. 2-11: It drops on you. Drink 5. 12-19: You push it off the ledge. Give 5 to whoever's still as stone. 20-20: Natural 20! You push it off the ledge. Give 10 to whoever's still as stone. · Jinx Stone Still: The cursed player sits perfectly still while anyone else rolls. Moves: drink 1. |

Drink 6. 2-6: Your frog wanders off. Drink 3. 7-19: Your frog wins. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! Your frog wins. Give 4. |
| ☐ | The Wishing Toad | `dungeon.mimic-fermenter` | challenge | A giant toad burps up prizes until it burps up you. Cash out or poke it again. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It swallows your hand. Drink 6. 2-6: It swallows your hand. Drink 3. 7-19: It burps. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! It burps. Give 4. |
| ☐ | The Bottled Spell | `dungeon.frozen-in-the-ice` | challenge | A spell sealed in a bottle, glowing. Uncork it, or leave it. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It's a curse. Drink 8. 2-11: It's a curse. Drink 4. 12-19: Something old and good. Give 4 to the oldest player here. 20-20: Natural 20! Something old and good. Give 8 to the oldest player here. |
| ☐ | The Locked Trunk | `dungeon.the-chest-freezer` | challenge | A wizard's trunk, scratched on the inside of the lid. Open it, or don't. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Something climbs out. Drink 8. 2-11: Something climbs out. Drink 4. 12-19: Robes and a lucky coin. Give 4 to whoever wants the robes. 20-20: Natural 20! Robes and a lucky coin. Give 8 to whoever wants the robes. · Jinx Hold Your Hat: The cursed player keeps one hand on their head. Lets go: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Scrying Pool | `dungeon.ice-fishing` | challenge | A pool that shows the future. Look, then cash out or look again, harder. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You see your future. Drink 8. 2-7: You see your future. Drink 4. 8-19: A glimpse of treasure. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! A glimpse of treasure. Give 6. |
| ☐ | Mimic Bookcase | `dungeon.mimic-fridge` | challenge | A bookcase with a heartbeat. It pays out until it bites. Cash out or open again. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It bites. Drink 8. 2-7: It bites. Drink 4. 8-19: A useful book. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! A useful book. Give 6. |
| ☐ | The Spyglass | `dungeon.the-telescope` | challenge | An old spyglass pointed at a castle far away. Look, or don't. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Someone looks back. Drink 10. 2-12: Someone looks back. Drink 5. 13-19: A flask hidden in the tube. Spy on someone; they drink 5. 20-20: Natural 20! A flask hidden in the tube. Spy on someone; they drink 10. |
| ☐ | The Watchtower | `dungeon.the-water-tower` | challenge | A ladder up an old watchtower, and something glinting at the top. Climb, or don't. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! A rung gives. Drink 10. 2-12: A rung gives. Drink 5. 13-19: The best view in the realm. Point at a far peak; give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! The best view in the realm. Point at a far peak; give 10. · Jinx Hydrate: The cursed player sips water between every drink. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | Sled Racing | `dungeon.paper-planes` | challenge | Bet on the sled races down the slope. Win, then cash out or bet again. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Yours flips. Drink 10. 2-8: Yours flips. Drink 5. 9-19: Yours wins. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! Yours wins. Give 8. |
| ☐ | Mimic Snowdrift | `dungeon.mimic-planter` | challenge | A snowdrift that grows prizes until it bites. Cash out or dig again. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It bites. Drink 10. 2-8: It bites. Drink 5. 9-19: Something glints. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! Something glints. Give 8. |
| ☐ | Sir Bradley's Charter | `dungeon.queue-knows-the-owner` | challenge | Sir Bradley has a charter from the king's cousin. Name a bigger patron, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! The cousin really is important. Drink 6. 2-7: The cousin really is important. Drink 3. 8-19: Nobody has heard of the cousin. Give 3 to whoever has the fanciest cousin. 20-20: Natural 20! Nobody has heard of the cousin. Give 6 to whoever has the fanciest cousin. |
| ☐ | Chadwick's Accent | `dungeon.queue-fake-id` | challenge | Chadwick says he's a foreign prince. You and he (left neighbor) do the accent. His wins: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | We're Together | `dungeon.queue-were-together` | challenge | Thaddeus tells the gate guard you're his squires. You're not. Roll 2d6 to argue. | 2d6 — 2-6: The guard believes him. Carry his bags and drink 3. 7-12: The guard believes you. Pick someone to be your squire; they drink 3. |
| ☐ | The Company Banner | `dungeon.queue-group-name` | challenge | The Gilded Company has a banner, a motto and matching capes. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Their capes are better. Drink 6. 2-7: Their capes are better. Drink 3. 8-19: You have a cape. It's a blanket, but still. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You have a cape. It's a blanket, but still. Give 6. · Jinx Champion: Everyone calls the cursed player 'Champion', like Sir Bradley does. Slip: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Roll of Heroes | `dungeon.queue-the-list` | challenge | Sir Bradley is on the roll of heroes. You're on a napkin the clerk is holding. Roll d6. | 1d6 — 1-1: The napkin blows away. Drink 3. 2-2: The clerk sneezes on it. Drink 3. 3-3: The napkin holds up. Give 2. 4-4: You're on the roll, and Bradley's spelled wrong. Give 3. 5-5: You're on it twice. Give 3. 6-6: Bradley's off the roll. Everyone else drinks 1. |
| ☐ | The Gate Toll | `dungeon.queue-cover-charge` | challenge | Chadwick tells the guard you're paying the toll for the whole Company. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! The guard believes him. Drink 6. 2-7: The guard believes him. Drink 3. 8-19: The guard charges Chadwick double. Give 3, twice, to the same person. 20-20: Natural 20! The guard charges Chadwick double. Give 6, twice, to the same person. |
| ☐ | The Royal Seal | `dungeon.queue-hand-stamp` | challenge | Thaddeus forged a royal seal out of a potato, and he's pressing it on you. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It works on you. Drink 6. 2-7: It works on you. Drink 3. 8-19: You wash it off in a horse trough. Seal someone's hand; they drink 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You wash it off in a horse trough. Seal someone's hand; they drink 6. |
| ☐ | Rock, Paper, Bradley | `dungeon.queue-rock-paper-brad` | challenge | Rock, paper, scissors with your left neighbor (Sir Bradley), best of three. Lose: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Chadwick's Shortcut | `dungeon.stairs-service-elevator` | challenge | Chadwick found a shortcut and is holding the gate shut behind him. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He wins. Take the long way and drink 8. 2-9: He wins. Take the long way and drink 4. 10-19: You squeeze through. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You squeeze through. Give 8. |
| ☐ | The Company Portrait | `dungeon.stairs-group-photo` | challenge | A painter is doing the Company's portrait across the whole road, and you're in it. Roll 2d6. | 2d6 — 2-6: You're painted out. Drink 4. 7-12: You pull a face in the background. Everyone poses; the worst pose drinks 4. |
| ☐ | Thaddeus's Speech | `dungeon.stairs-pep-talk` | challenge | Thaddeus gives a battle speech. Give your table a better one in 10 seconds. Worse: drink 4. |  |
| ☐ | The Kill Count | `dungeon.stairs-leaderboard` | challenge | Sir Bradley keeps a tally of monsters slain, and you're second. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He pulls ahead. Drink 8. 2-9: He pulls ahead. Drink 4. 10-19: You overtake him. Give 4 to whoever's last. 20-20: Natural 20! You lap him twice. Everyone else drinks 3. |
| ☐ | The Recruitment | `dungeon.stairs-recruitment` | challenge | The Company wants you. Thaddeus is holding out a gold cape. Roll d20 to say no. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You say yes. Drink 8. 2-9: You say yes. Drink 4. 10-19: You say no and mean it. Throw someone a cape; they drink 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You say no and mean it. Throw someone a cape; they drink 8. · Jinx Company Man: The cursed player ends every sentence with 'huzzah'. Slip: drink 1. |
| ☐ | First Through the Door | `dungeon.closing-last-table` | challenge | The Company wants the dragon first. Sir Bradley is already in the doorway. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He elbows you aside. Drink 8. 2-11: He elbows you aside. Drink 4. 12-19: You trip him, gently. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You trip him into the moat. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: Bradley wants the doorway. On go, you and he (left neighbor) stand, spin, sit. Last down: drink 4. (Won/Lost) |
| ☐ | A Toast to the Company | `dungeon.closing-the-boys-toast` | challenge | Thaddeus is toasting 'the Company', eleven minutes in. Roll d20 to cut him off. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You cry at it. Finish your drink. 2-11: It's moving, somehow. Raise a glass and drink 4. 12-19: You raise a glass mid-sentence, and it's over. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You raise a glass mid-sentence, and it's over. Give 8. · Beat 2: Your turn: toast the Gilded Company, sincerely, for ten seconds. Laugh: drink 4. (Won/Lost) |
| ☐ | Chadwick's Potions | `dungeon.closing-last-round` | challenge | Chadwick bought the last healing potions, for the Company only. Steal one. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He drinks yours in front of you. Drink 8. 2-11: He drinks yours in front of you. Drink 4. 12-19: You swap his for pond water. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You swap them all. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: Chadwick grabs the potion back. Roll 2d6. (2d6 — 2-7: He spills it on you. Drink 4. 8-12: It's yours. Give 4.) · Jinx For the Company: The cursed player says 'for the Company' before every drink. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | Sir Bradley's Invoice | `dungeon.closing-venmo-request` | challenge | Sir Bradley sends everyone here an invoice for 'inspiration'. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You pay it. Drink 8. 2-11: You pay it. Drink 4. 12-19: You invoice him back, double. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You invoice him back, double. Give 8. · Beat 2: Pay Bradley (your left neighbor) back in compliments: three in ten seconds. Fall short: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Jinx I Owe You: The cursed player says 'I'll pay you back' when given a drink. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Victory Dance | `dungeon.closing-dance-off` | challenge | The Company starts its victory dance before the fight. Out-dance them. Roll d6. | 1d6 — 1-1: Bradley does the worm in full plate. Drink 4. 2-2: You pull something. Drink 4. 3-3: Thaddeus dips you. You let him. Drink 4. 4-4: You do a sword dance, and mean it. Give 4. 5-5: You moonwalk past the dragon's door and back. Give 4. 6-6: The dragon taps a claw along. Everyone else drinks 2. · Beat 2: Round two: you and Thaddeus (left neighbor) dance, no music. The table votes. He wins: drink 4. (Won/Lost) |
| ☐ | The Company's Debts | `dungeon.closing-boys-tab` | challenge | Sir Bradley signs the Company's debts over to you. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Signed. Drink 8. 2-11: Signed. Drink 4. 12-19: You sign it 'Bradley'. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You sign it 'Bradley'. Give 8. · Beat 2: The moneylender wants a signature. Roll d6. (1d6 — 1-1: You sign it. Drink 4. 2-2: You sign it twice. Drink 4. 3-3: You tip him too. Drink 3. 4-4: You sign it 'Bradley'. Give 3. 5-5: You sign it 'Chadwick'. Give 4. 6-6: You sign it 'The Company'. Everyone else drinks 2.) |
| ☐ | Thaddeus's Horse | `dungeon.closing-thads-uber` | challenge | Thaddeus brought one horse for everyone, and it's a donkey. Roll 2d6 for a seat. | 2d6 — 2-7: You're behind the donkey. Drink 4. 8-12: In the saddle. Give 4. · Beat 2: Dibs on the saddle: you and Thaddeus (left neighbor) call it. Table judges. He's first: drink 4. (Won/Lost) |
| ☐ | The Rematch | `dungeon.closing-the-rematch` | challenge | Chadwick wants a rematch for the city gate. He's been stretching since. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He's been training. Drink 8. 2-11: He's been training. Drink 4. 12-19: Same result as the gate. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! Chadwick is so impressed he knights you. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: Thumb war with Chadwick (your left neighbor), best of three. Lose: drink 4. (Won/Lost) |
| ☐ | The Gingerbread Door | `dungeon.peephole` | challenge | A gingerbread door wants a password. Invent one in 5 seconds and sell it. Table says no: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Witch's Cat | `dungeon.flapper-fran` | challenge | A black cat blocks the path and judges you. Do your best cat for 5 seconds, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It's not impressed. Drink 6. 2-7: It's not impressed. Drink 3. 8-19: It purrs and lets you by. Scratch someone behind the ears; they drink 3. 20-20: Natural 20! It purrs and lets you by. Scratch someone behind the ears; they drink 6. |
| ☐ | The Cauldron | `dungeon.bathtub-gin` | challenge | A cauldron of something green, and a ladle. Roll d6. | 1d6 — 1-1: It tastes like feet. Drink 3. 2-2: You turn green for a minute. Drink 3. 3-3: Smooth, somehow. Give 2. 4-4: You ladle out a round. Give 3. 5-5: You find the witch's recipe book. Give 3. 6-6: You bottle it and sell it. Everyone else drinks 1. |
| ☐ | Granny Hex | `dungeon.the-proprietor` | challenge | Three hundred years old, and she has never lost a staring contest. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! She turns you into a toad, briefly. Drink 8. 2-9: She turns you into a toad, briefly. Drink 4. 10-19: You knock the broom out of her hand. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You steal the broom and the goat. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: She wants the goat back. Haggle with your left neighbor for ten seconds. Table says no: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Jinx Ribbit: The cursed player says 'ribbit' after every sip. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Toll Booth | `dungeon.bottle-service` | challenge | The bandits charge a toll to get in, and another to get out. Roll 2d6. | 2d6 — 2-6: You pay both. Drink 3. 7-12: You pay in fake coins. Give 3 to whoever looks richest. |

| ☐ | The Gatekeeper | `dungeon.the-bodyguard` | challenge | Seven feet tall, one eye, and he wants the password. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Wrong password. Drink 6. 2-7: Wrong password. Drink 3. 8-19: You walk in like you own it. Give 3 to whoever's royalty here. 20-20: Natural 20! You walk in like you own it. Give 6 to whoever's royalty here. |
| ☐ | One-Eyed Mag | `dungeon.the-promoter` | challenge | The bandit chief. The strongbox is under her chair, and she's sitting on it. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! She recruits you. Finish your drink. 2-9: She robs you too. Drink 4. 10-19: You take the strongbox. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You take the strongbox. Give 8. · Beat 2: She wants a duel of insults. Trade three with your left neighbor; the table judges. Lose: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Jinx Bandit's Honor: Nobody speaks to the cursed player except through someone else. Slip: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Barrow Wight | `dungeon.the-accountant` | challenge | A dead lord in rusted armor, counting his coins, and you. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You're counted. Drink 6. 2-7: You're counted. Drink 3. 8-19: You knock his helmet off. Give 3 to whoever owes you money. 20-20: Natural 20! You knock his helmet off. Give 6 to whoever owes you money. |
| ☐ | The Stone Watcher | `dungeon.security-camera` | challenge | Freeze like a statue for 15 seconds while the table tries to crack you. Move: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Sealed Tomb | `dungeon.the-safe` | challenge | The seal is a riddle nobody remembers. Roll d6 to crack it. | 1d6 — 1-1: The curse goes off. Drink 3. 2-2: Dust in your eyes. Drink 3. 3-3: Empty, but you look brave. Give 2. 4-4: A few old coins. Give 3. 5-5: Coins, and a jeweled cup. Give 3. 6-6: It was a king's tomb. Everyone else drinks 1. |
| ☐ | The Barrow King | `dungeon.the-manager` | challenge | A crown, a sword, and no patience for visitors. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He buries you, a little. Drink 8. 2-9: He buries you, a little. Drink 4. 10-19: You knock his crown off. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You take the crown. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: He demands your lineage: name four ancestors, real or not, in ten seconds. Fail: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Jinx Grave Manners: The cursed player bows before every sentence. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Merfolk Gambler | `dungeon.card-sharp` | challenge | He deals you a hand of wet cards and smiles too wide. Roll 2d6 for your hand. | 2d6 — 2-6: His hand is better. Drink 3. 7-12: Full house. Give 3. |
| ☐ | The Siren | `dungeon.the-tell` | challenge | The siren only trusts liars. Tell two truths and a lie. Anyone spots the lie: you drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Drowned Priest | `dungeon.the-dealer` | challenge | He has prayed underwater for a hundred years and wants you to join him. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He blesses you, wetly. Drink 6. 2-7: He blesses you, wetly. Drink 3. 8-19: You ring his bell. Bless someone; they drink 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You ring his bell. Bless someone; they drink 6. |
| ☐ | The Sea Witch | `dungeon.big-mike` | challenge | She trades in voices and has never lost a bargain. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! She takes your voice. Drink 8. 2-9: She takes your voice. Drink 4. 10-19: You crack her shell. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You take her voice instead. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: She sings. The table has ten seconds to make you laugh. Laugh: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Jinx Voiceless: The cursed player may only whisper. Speaks up: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Last Guard | `dungeon.arm-wrestle` | challenge | The vault's last guard wants a test of strength. Arm-wrestle your right neighbor. Lose: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Rune Door | `dungeon.the-ref` | challenge | A door covered in runes that change when you look away. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! The runes bite. Drink 6. 2-7: The runes bite. Drink 3. 8-19: It swings open. Hold the door for someone; they drink 3. 20-20: Natural 20! It swings open. Hold the door for someone; they drink 6. |
| ☐ | The Cave Troll | `dungeon.the-undercard` | challenge | A young cave troll, warming up on you. Roll d6. | 1d6 — 1-1: Club, club, club. Drink 3. 2-2: Headlock. Drink 3. 3-3: You slip the club. Give 2. 4-4: You counter. Give 3. 5-5: You knock it down the stairs. Give 3. 6-6: The dwarves cheer. Everyone else drinks 1. |
| ☐ | The Deep Worm | `dungeon.the-undefeated` | challenge | A worm the size of a hall, coiled around the dwarf's gold. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It swallows you and spits you out. Finish your drink. 2-9: It flattens you. Drink 4. 10-19: You hit it with a dwarf's hammer. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You hit it with a dwarf's hammer. Give 8. · Beat 2: It coils for another strike. Arm-wrestle your left neighbor. Lose: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Jinx Deep Silence: Nobody says the word 'gold'. Slip: drink 1. |
| ☐ | A Week of Rain | `dungeon.omen-flooded-stairs` | rule | Last to say 'squelch' as a region opens drinks 1. Traps hurt 1 more. Take a Smoke Bomb. |  |
| ☐ | Harvest Festival | `dungeon.omen-happy-hour` | rule | Feasts in every village: whoever drinks picks someone to drink with them. Heals heal 1 more. |  |
| ☐ | The King's Advance | `dungeon.omen-running-a-tab` | rule | The king pays up front: drink 1. Start two hearts short, with a Tower Shield and a Healing Potion. |  |
| ☐ | Curfew | `dungeon.omen-lock-in` | rule | Ask to leave your seat, or drink 2. Bosses take an extra hit. Take a Lucky Coin and a shield. |  |
| ☐ | A Bottomless Bag | `dungeon.omen-cargo-shorts` | rule | A bag bigger on the inside: drink 3. One more satchel slot. |  |
| ☐ | Butterfingers | `dungeon.omen-butterfingers` | rule | Anyone who drops anything drinks 2. One satchel slot fewer, but two more hearts. |  |
| ☐ | Blood Moon | `dungeon.omen-blood-moon` | rule | Everyone howls at the red moon; last to howl drinks 2. Fights cost a heart more; two more hearts. |  |
| ☐ | The Oath | `dungeon.omen-lefty-night` | rule | Everyone swears to drink only with their off hand all quest. Caught using the other: drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | Battle Hymns | `dungeon.omen-karaoke-night` | rule | Before every boss swing, the drawer sings one line of a battle hymn. Refuse: drink 2. |  |
| ☐ | The Town Cheers | `dungeon.omen-the-regulars` | rule | The whole town sees you off. Everyone toasts the person on their left. Two more hearts. |  |
| ☐ | A Sleeping Giant | `dungeon.omen-thin-walls` | rule | A giant sleeps nearby: everyone whispers. Trials cost a heart more; heals heal one more. |  |
| ☐ | The Farewell Toast | `dungeon.omen-cold-open` | rule | Everyone drinks 1 at the city gate. Start a heart short, with a Lucky Coin and a Tower Shield. |  |
| ☐ | A Treasure Map | `dungeon.omen-payday` | rule | A treasure map, maybe a fake. Gambles and relics roll 2 higher. Start a heart short. |  |
| ☐ | The Village Healer | `dungeon.omen-designated-driver` | rule | The village healer packs you a potion. One more satchel slot, but bosses cost a heart more. |  |
| ☐ | A Black Cat | `dungeon.omen-friday-13th` | rule | A black cat crossed your path, twice. Natural 1s land on a 2 too, natural 20s on a 19. |  |
| ☐ | The Riddling Moon | `dungeon.omen-trivia-night` | rule | Before each boss, the drawer answers the table's riddle, or drinks 2. Trials cost less, traps more. |  |
| ☐ | Bandit Season | `dungeon.omen-brads-birthday` | rule | It's bandit season, and they've been practicing. Ambushes cost a heart more. Take a Tower Shield. |  |
| ☐ | The Royal Wedding | `dungeon.omen-open-bar` | rule | A royal wedding, and the wine is free. Take two Healing Potions, but one satchel slot fewer. |  |
| ☐ | Heroic Names | `dungeon.omen-name-tags` | rule | Everyone picks a heroic name at the gate and answers only to it. Wrong name: drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | A Falling Star | `dungeon.omen-lucky-penny` | rule | Everyone wished on a falling star. Every d20 fight rolls 1 higher, but start two hearts short. |  |
| ☐ | Barbarian | `dungeon.class-barbarian` | rule | Rage first. Questions never. +1 to monster d20s. +2 to boss d20s. Traps hurt 1 more. |  |
| ☐ | Rogue | `dungeon.class-rogue` | rule | Took that. +2 to trap and relic d20s. +1 to ambush d20s. +1 satchel slot. Starts with a Smoke Bomb. |  |
| ☐ | Cleric | `dungeon.class-cleric` | rule | Blesses every drink, loudly. +1 max heart. Every heal heals 1 more. |  |
| ☐ | Wizard | `dungeon.class-wizard` | rule | Smells of burnt eyebrows. −1 max heart. Starts with a Lucky Coin. |  |
| ☐ | Bard | `dungeon.class-bard` | rule | Brought a lute. Nobody asked. +1 max heart. Natural 20 on a 19 or 20. Every heal heals 1 more. |  |
| ☐ | Paladin | `dungeon.class-paladin` | rule | Sworn to protect, loudly. +1 max heart. +1 to boss d20s. Starts with a Tower Shield. |  |
| ☐ | Ranger | `dungeon.class-ranger` | rule | Hasn't slept indoors in years. +2 to monster d20s. +1 to ambush d20s. Bosses hurt 1 more. |  |
| ☐ | Drunken Monk | `dungeon.class-monk` | rule | Starts drunk. Gets better. −1 max heart. Natural 1 on a 1 or 2. |  |
| ☐ | Necromancer | `dungeon.class-necromancer` | rule | Has died twice this week already. −3 max hearts. +1 to boss d20s. Starts with a Phoenix Feather. |  |
| ☐ | Alchemist | `dungeon.class-alchemist` | rule | Has a flask for every flask. +1 max heart. +1 satchel slot. Starts with 2 Healing Potions. |  |
| ☐ | Warlock | `dungeon.class-warlock` | rule | Read none of the pact. −2 max hearts. +2 to every battle d20. |  |
| ☐ | Knight | `dungeon.class-bouncer` | rule | Mentions the armor daily. +2 max hearts. +1 to monster d20s. Ambushes hurt 1 less. |  |
| ☐ | Innkeeper | `dungeon.class-bartender` | rule | Knows every rumor. +1 max heart. +1 satchel slot. Starts with a Hired Sword and a Healing Potion. |  |
| ☐ | Sellsword | `dungeon.class-mercenary` | rule | Will fight anything for coin. +1 max heart. +1 to monster d20s. +3 to elite d20s. |  |
| ☐ | Druid | `dungeon.class-druid` | rule | Talks to the trees. The trees talk back. +1 max heart. +2 to trap d20s. Every heal heals 1 more. |  |
| ☐ | The Peasant | `dungeon.class-plus-one` | rule | Brought dad's pitchfork. −3 max hearts. −1 to boss d20s. Every 20 is rerolled. Traps hurt 1 more. |  |
| ☐ | The Squire | `dungeon.class-intern` | rule | Drops things. −2 max hearts. Natural 1 on a 1 or 2. Every heal heals 1 less. −2 satchel slots. |  |
| ☐ | The Mimic | `dungeon.class-mimic` | rule | A very confused chest. −5 max hearts. −1 to every battle d20. −1 to monster d20s. +2 satchel slots. |  |

## Notes for review

- Dice cards are ordinary cards with a `dice` definition (which dice plus how
  to read the total); the engine pauses the deck until the roll is resolved.
- Secret cards show the secret only while a player holds the card's plaque;
  a fuse burns for a hidden time in its range, a clock counts down in view,
  and the card can't be put aside until time is up.
- Rules target 90 characters / 18 words; the build warns above that and
  rejects over 100 characters / 20 words.
- Rank cards for 9 / 10 / Jack / Queen are intentionally covered by the
  existing Rhyme Time, Categories, Rulemaster and Questions Only cards.
